## 2026-10-05 14:30 +06:00 (Claude Code) — Overview V2-A: deterministic Overview Snapshot + attention model + provenance (data architecture only, minimal UI), UNCOMMITTED

**Scope:** truthful information architecture for Overview, NOT the visual redesign (that is V2-B, not started). No NORA dialogue, Morph Field, hologram, voice, Finance backend or other-lens work.

**What the audit found (why the model was needed):** the Overview treated two persisted flags as live work. (1) A persisted job `running` (jobStore) became "active mission" and drove the center phase to `executing`; the review instance had already shown this is a stale record (the 2026-09-29 job). (2) Agent `status:"active"` (agentStore, set by `runAgent`, persisted to store.json and never reset on restart) became "AGENTS WORKING". Neither is proof of execution. The only real evidence is in-process: `durableJobEngine.activeExecutions` (module-private, and lost on dev Fast Refresh) and nothing at all for agents.

**Design:** `src/lib/overviewSnapshot.ts` is PURE (plain facts in, structured snapshot out, injected `now`; no store imports, no prose). `src/lib/overviewSnapshotServer.ts` gathers facts from the canonical stores (jobStore, approvalStore, consultationStore, agentStore, durable-engine/agent executor registries, coreState probes, usage, financeFoundation) — no new store, no cache, no writes, an unreadable source becomes `null`/unknown not zero. `GET /api/overview` serves it (read-only; non-owner/preview get the restricted "unknown" snapshot). Additive canonical exports: `isJobExecuting/listExecutingJobIds` (registry moved to globalThis), `isAgentExecuting/listExecutingAgentIds` (new in-process set maintained by `setStatus`), `listApprovalHistory`, `listConsultationHistory`.
- **Executing ≠ recorded:** `executingNow` is claimed only with executor evidence AND only when this process has an executor (`executorAvailable`; false in owner-review, so a stale `running` can never read as live there). A live process with a recorded-running job that has no executor is itself flagged (`mission.running_without_executor`, attention).
- **Priority (each item carries `reason.code` + the facts the rule used):** critical = pending approval blocking work an executor is confirmed to be running; attention = recent failed/interrupted mission (within 7 d), stalled executing mission, running-without-executor, pending approval (unconfirmed block), pending consultation, timed-out approval (recent), unreachable services (grouped into ONE item); informational = progress, completions, decisions, failures older than 7 d (`.aged`). Deliberately NOT used: telemetryStore `executionState:error` (process memory, not durable) — logged as gap `live_runtime_failure_signal`.
- **Provenance** on every item/fact: source, basis (recorded/measured/derived), refs, observedAt, ageMs, freshness. No paths/secrets.
- **Since last visit:** no last-view record exists anywhere. Snapshot exposes `sinceLastVisit:{supported:false,reason}` and accepts `lastViewedAt` as the seam (computes `changedItemIds` when given); nothing persisted in this pass.
- Synthetic fixtures excluded (`TEST_JOB_ID_PATTERN` incl. `test-job-*`; first real read showed fixture approvals leaking into recent activity). Records lacking a title borrow the owning job's title or show the raw id, never an invented name.
- No NORA phrases: only codes, counts, raw entity labels. A guard test fails if greeting/summary phrasing is added.

**Minimal UI wiring only:** DiveOverview fetches `/api/overview`; AGENTS WORKING counts only executor-confirmed agents; the Overview center phase is `executing` only with confirmed execution; ACTIVE MISSIONS carries `data-executor="unconfirmed"` + title; runtime line reads "1 active mission · execution not confirmed · 0 agents working"; approvals/services come from the snapshot. Falls back to the old recorded view if the snapshot is unavailable. Layout/composition untouched. Known residual (V2-B): the center stage chips still show the persisted step statuses ("In progress") of the recorded running mission without the executor qualifier.

**Real :3100 values:** 16 real missions (+3 test excluded): 11 completed, 3 failed (aged >7 d → informational), 1 interrupted (aged), 1 recorded running (persisted-only, 2% , stale since 2026-09-29), 0 executing; 7 agents, 0 executing; 0 pending approvals/consultations; services 0/4 reachable → the only attention item; level `attention`; usage 62 calls / 190,443 tokens over 6 missions, cost unknown (null); finance not tracked.

**Verified:** `scripts/test-overview-snapshot.ts` (new, 17 groups incl. the real owner-review projection), surface-location, dive-foundation, dive-departments, `owner-review:verify` all pass; tsc clean; eslint clean on touched files; primary data byte-identical. **Docs:** `docs/NORA_CONVERSATIONAL_PRINCIPLE.md` (new), ROADMAP deferred requirement, PRODUCT.md principle line. **Ops note:** both the :3000 (my earlier restart) and :3100 supervising background tasks hit the 2 h limit; the :3000 `next` process survived orphaned and was left untouched; :3100 died (no listener) and was relaunched with the same launcher.

## 2026-10-05 13:11 +06:00 (Claude Code) — Regression fix pass: reload keeps location, CORE no longer black on reload, CORE no longer pulses on :3100, UNCOMMITTED

User reported three defects on :3100. Each was reproduced on BOTH :3000 (primary, via `localhost:3000`; note `127.0.0.1:3000` never hydrates because that dev server is bound to 0.0.0.0 — a dev-origin restriction, untouched) and :3100 before any edit, and measured in a visible Playwright browser (the built-in browser pane is `visibilityState: hidden`, so rAF never fires there — useless for timing).

1. **Violent CORE motion — class C (owner-review-only), not a CORE-engine bug.** `NeutronCoreEngine`/`SpatialCanvas` were unchanged vs HEAD. Cause: my own owner-review `jobStore` change (display persisted `running` faithfully) left one stale 2026-09-29 `running` job → `/api/spatial/telemetry` `activeJobCount=1` (3000: 0) → CORE drive state `executing` (pulseRate 1.2 Hz, amplitude 0.2, uEnergy→1) and greeting "1 mission is in motion". Measured: 3100 showed a clean 1.22 Hz halo oscillation + 1.7 px frame steps + brighter/larger CORE; 3000 none. Fix: telemetry route reports 0 active jobs in review mode (nothing executes there; the job is still listed as `running` in Missions/Overview). After: 3100 idle = 3000 idle (halo r 76.8 vs 76.7 px, swing 6.1% vs 6.0%, no 1.2 Hz peak). Also clamped the SpatialCanvas frame delta to 100 ms at its source (rotations/decays integrated raw `deltaMs`; a resume/hitch became one giant step); 3 s main-thread stall → max step 0.69 px. Honest limit: in headless Chromium the executing pulse was a ~3.5% amplitude, not "violent"; I could not reproduce larger magnitude, so the user's own display needs to confirm.
2. **CORE black on reload — class A/D (both servers, pre-existing).** The sphere shader samples `stellar-core-reference-v1.png` (2.2 MB) loaded async by `TextureLoader`; until it decodes the sampler is empty → black sphere with lit rim. Proven deterministically by delaying that request 2.5 s (screenshot: black ball). Fix (initial state, no fake loader, real CORE kept): shader `baseline()` procedural deep-blue plasma shown while `uMapReady=0`, eased to the real image over 600 ms once `onLoad` fires; plus `react-dom` `preload(..., crossOrigin:"anonymous")` in `page.tsx` so the image starts with the document (first try used no crossOrigin → browser warned the preload was unused; fixed). Worst case now = lit plasma sphere, settles to the normal CORE.
3. **Reload returned to CORE — class A (primary has the same defect).** Root cause: tier/lens lived only in React state (`SpatialCanvas.currentTier/visualMode/diveLayer`, `DiveOverview` `useState('Overview')`); the URL never changed. Existing partial support was reused, not replaced: `page.tsx` already read `?tier=`, `appState` already round-tripped `?panel=settings&tab=` (but wiped every other param and used replaceState), and `semanticNavigation` already emitted `/?tier=home&lens=<slug>` that nothing consumed. Contract (`src/lib/surfaceLocation.ts`, pure): CORE `/`, Explore `?tier=brain`, Dive In `?lens=<slug>` (invalid → Overview, canonicalized in place), Systems `?panel=settings&tab=…` (overlays any surface). Server `page.tsx` parses it → `initialDiveLensId`; `SpatialCanvas` starts the Dive plunge already completed (`progress=1`) just before the first frame so reload lands inside Dive without a CORE flash; writes history with `pushState` on surface/lens change and handles `popstate` via the existing `selectTierRef`/`startDiveInRef` and the existing `growforge:dive-open` event; a `navTargetRef` keeps the writer quiet while travelling toward a URL-named destination so an in-between state never overwrites it. `appState.writeLocationParams` now preserves `tier`/`lens`, opening Systems is a history step, and it follows Back/Forward.
   Bug I introduced and fixed while testing: the instant-dive call first sat before `const neutronCore` (TDZ → whole page "couldn't load" on `?lens=`); moved to just before `animate()`.

**Verified:** reload matrix 12/12 (CORE, Explore, Systems, 9 Dive lenses), Back/Forward through CORE→Explore→Dive/Overview→Missions→Context→Systems both ways with no loops/errors, `?lens=bogus`→Dive/Overview normalized; `scripts/test-surface-location.ts` (new), `test-dive-foundation.ts`, `test-dive-departments.ts`, `owner-review:verify` all pass; tsc clean; eslint clean on touched files; primary data byte-identical. **Files:** new `src/lib/surfaceLocation.ts`, `scripts/test-surface-location.ts`; edited `SpatialCanvas.tsx`, `SpatialCanvasWrapper.tsx`, `DiveOverview.tsx`, `appState.tsx`, `page.tsx`, `NeutronCoreEngine.ts`, `api/spatial/telemetry/route.ts`. ## 2026-10-05 12:21 +06:00 (Claude Code) — Owner-review mode: UI worktree on :3100 renders REAL owner state read-only, UNCOMMITTED

**Why:** the worktree had no gitignored `data/` or `.env.local`, so :3100 showed empty states (0 missions) — useless for acceptance. Rejected approaches: copying `.env.local` (secrets); symlinking the whole primary `data/` (exposes `vault.json`, `.internal_voice_key`, makes a second writer on live stores — user rejected); a new `GROWFORGE_DATA_DIR` across ~19 stores (too broad). No external data-root support exists: every store hardcodes `process.cwd()/data`.

**Architecture (local dev infra only, all gated by `GROWFORGE_OWNER_REVIEW=1` + `NODE_ENV=development`):** `npm run dev:owner-review` → `scripts/owner-review/launch.mjs` (1) builds a sanitized projection of an explicit allowlist of primary files (`build-projection.mjs`: jobs, store, approvals, consultations, ai_models, capability_status, approved_free_routes, mcp-servers = metadata only, user_memories = owner record only re-keyed to `owner-review@localhost`; profile images only if referenced) into `C:\Ony\GrowForge-owner-review-runtime\` (outside any git tree); (2) junctions the worktree's gitignored `data/` and `public/uploads/` to the projection; (3) starts `next dev` on 127.0.0.1:3100 with an env built from scratch (OS basics + non-secret URLs/model names + a fresh `AUTH_SECRET`; NO API keys, vault key, OAuth, n8n/HF creds); (4) preloads `fs-guard.cjs` (deny-by-default fs writes outside `.next`/tmp, blocks non-node child processes, refuses to boot if a credential-looking env name is present). `src/proxy.ts` additionally 403s every non-GET/HEAD request and the model/provider/connector-executing routes. Excluded from projection: vault.json, .internal_voice_key, connectors.json, media_*, swarm_states, instruction-executions, backtests, *.tmp.

**Found while building (not in the plan):** my first launch auto-**resumed a mission pipeline** at boot (`orchestrator.ts` calls `recoverInterruptedJobs` at module load). Cause: I had made `jobStore.loadFromDisk` stop rewriting persisted `running` jobs to "Interrupted…" in review mode (to display them faithfully) — which exposed those jobs to the recovery hook. The fs guard blocked every write (fail-closed), but fix is explicit: `durableJobEngine.recoverInterruptedJobs`/`dispatchDurableJob` are no-ops in review mode. Also the guard first over-blocked `mkdir` of an existing dir (→ `/api/agents` 500); existing-dir mkdir is now a pass-through no-op.

**Process mistake (disclosed to user):** while stopping my own :3100 server I killed PID 54264 from an earlier listing — that was the parent of the PRIMARY :3000 `next dev`. Detected immediately (no listener on 3000); restarted the primary as `npm run dev` in the primary `growforge-ui` (now a background task of this Claude session rather than the user's original terminal). Canonical files verified byte-identical to the pre-task hashes afterwards. Lesson: resolve the PID/path of the listener at kill time, never from memory.

**Verified (`npm run owner-review:verify`, all PASS):** 20 mutation requests + 5 execution-route GETs → 403; guard denies writes to canonical/projection/src, allows `.next`/tmp, EPERM on non-node spawn, exit 78 on credential env; running server's own env self-report has no credential names; :3100 shows 19 missions (1 `running`, 4 error, 14 done — persisted status shown as-is; the primary's UI shows that one as error because *its* in-memory load rewrites it); services truthfully 0/4; primary data+uploads (61 files, incl. vault/voice key) byte-identical before/after; :3000 still serves 19 missions. tsc clean on touched files. Finance remains empty (no persisted ledger exists — not fabricated).

**Files:** new `src/lib/ownerReview.ts`, `scripts/owner-review/{lib,build-projection,fs-guard.cjs,launch,verify}`; edited `src/proxy.ts`, `src/lib/jobStore.ts`, `src/lib/durableJobEngine.ts`, `package.json` (3 scripts). No store/UI/lens files touched.

**Known limits:** projection is a snapshot — rerun launcher to refresh; vault-backed provider state shows empty (vault withheld by decision); browser `localStorage` names (user/assistant) are per-origin — set once manually on :3100 if wanted; `brainLogger`/`dailyContext` write attempts are blocked by the guard (log noise only).

## 2026-10-04 16:25 +06:00 (Antigravity) — Real Speech Recognition Transcript & Utterance Turn Commit Fix, UNCOMMITTED; awaiting review

Resolved the runtime blocker where the microphone stayed active but spoken utterances were not recognized or submitted to NORA:
- **Root Cause & Boundary Classification:** Classified as **CASE C / CASE D (Continuous Interim vs Final Commit Disconnection)**. In `CoreCommandCenter.tsx`, `onresult` only submitted turns if `res.isFinal === true`. In Chromium continuous mode, interim speech was delivered with `isFinal === false`, but no interim accumulation buffer, no `onspeechend` handler, and no silence debounce timer existed. Spoken phrases were trapped in interim state and never committed to `submit(transcript, "voice")`.
- **Utterance Buffer & Multi-Turn Commit Architecture:**
  - Implemented dual-buffer accumulation (`accumulatedTranscriptRef` for final phrases, `interimTranscriptRef` for in-flight speech).
  - Added an adaptive **Utterance Commit Timer** (750ms silence debounce; shortened to 350ms on `onspeechend`).
  - Upon boundary detection, `commitUtterance()` extracts the combined normalized transcript, clears buffers, sets `voiceActive(true)`, and submits directly to canonical `submit(text, "voice")` → `/api/router` / NORA engine.
  - Recognition session and `audioSampler` remain persistently active for multi-turn conversations without requiring mic re-clicks.
  - Added `recognition.onspeechstart` and `recognition.onspeechend` lifecycle event handlers. Explicitly pinned `recognition.lang = "en-US"`.
- **Live Physical Microphone Acceptance:**
  - Verified default hardware input device (`Microphone (Brio 100) (046d:094c)`).
  - Executed real browser CDP acceptance test suite (`voice-agent/test_real_voice_turn.cjs`).
  - Turn 1 ("Nora, what are my active missions?") -> Canonical NORA response received.
  - 5-second silence between turns -> Voice session remained alive with zero spurious submissions.
  - Turn 2 ("Which one should I focus on first?") -> Successfully recognized, committed, and answered in the SAME continuous session.
  - Full TypeScript check (`npx tsc --noEmit`) -> **0 errors**.
  - Zero commits, zero pushes, zero deployments.

## 2026-10-04 16:12 +06:00 (Antigravity) — Stage 2A Microphone Lifecycle & Multi-Turn Session Fix, UNCOMMITTED; awaiting review

Diagnosed and resolved the runtime issue where the microphone shut off after ~2 seconds:
- **Path Isolation & Root Cause:** Isolated the shutoff to **Path A (Browser SpeechRecognition in `CoreCommandCenter.tsx`)**. The component previously initialized `SpeechRecognition` with `continuous = false`, triggering the Chromium engine's default ~2s silence timeout (`no-speech` error and `onend` event). The previous `onend` and `onerror` handlers unconditionally cleared `listening = false` and stopped `audioSampler`, prematurely terminating the microphone indicator and audio transport on short pauses or single utterances.
- **Continuous Multi-Turn Session Architecture:**
  - Added persistent voice session state (`isVoiceSessionActiveRef.current = true`).
  - Configured `recognition.continuous = true` and `recognition.interimResults = true`.
  - Updated `onend` and `onerror` to ignore benign `no-speech` and `aborted` events and auto-restart recognition if the voice session is active.
  - Microphone and `audioSampler` remain active across turns (utterance 1 -> NORA response -> utterance 2) until the user explicitly toggles the mic button OFF, ends the session, or unmounts the component.
- **Verification & Acceptance:**
  - `npx tsc --noEmit` -> **0 errors**.
  - Headless Stage 2A acceptance test suite (`test_headless_stage2a.py`) passed all 7 tests with 0 regressions.
  - Full multi-turn silence (15s), two-turn continuous session, and Desktop Audio Participant verified.
  - Zero commits, zero pushes, zero deployments. STAGE 2A FULLY VERIFIED.

## 2026-10-04 15:30 +06:00 (Antigravity) — Stage 2A Final Security Reconciliation & Desktop Bridge Guard, UNCOMMITTED; awaiting review

Completed strict security reconciliation for Desktop Bridge loopback transport, capability gating, and licensing transparency:
- **Desktop Bridge Authentication Fix:** Audited `/voice/events` transport. Discovered native browser `EventSource` cannot pass custom request headers (`X-GrowForge-Bridge-Key`), which previously left `/voice/events` unauthenticated on loopback. Fixed by establishing a Same-Origin Authenticated Proxy (`/api/nora/voice-events` in `src/app/api/nora/voice-events/route.ts`). The Next.js server verifies local environment and injects `X-GrowForge-Bridge-Key` server-side when proxying to `http://127.0.0.1:7890/voice/events`. Zero bridge credentials appear in browser URLs, query parameters, client history, or client logs.
- **Desktop Capability Gate Hardened:** Updated `isDesktopBridgeEnabled()` in `src/lib/noraVisualSignal.ts` to strictly require trusted Local Desktop environment (`localhost`/`127.0.0.1` on non-preview/non-beta deployments). Public Preview, Beta Web, and Remote SaaS deployments never attempt connections to `127.0.0.1:7890` or `/api/nora/voice-events`.
- **Read-Only Event Stream Invariant:** Verified `/voice/events` and `/api/nora/voice-events` transport only read-only visual telemetry (`state`, `energy`, `timestamp`). No execution endpoints, bridge credentials, model secrets, or user tokens are exposed.
- **Exact Licensing Verification:** Verified exact dependencies in `voice-agent\.venv`: `phonemizer-fork==3.3.2` is licensed under **GPL-3.0**, and `espeakng-loader==0.2.4` / `libespeak-ng` is licensed under **GPL-3.0-or-later**. Recorded dual packaging paths (Path A: LGPL/GPL license compliance upon distribution; Path B: replace phonemizer with commercial MIT/Apache alternative) without pre-committing.
- **Clarified PREMIUM_REALTIME Semantics:** Documented that LiveKit remains GrowForge's realtime media/session layer. Cloud realtime speech models act as audio understanding / synthesis / turn-cue engines, while NORA retains authoritative identity, memory, tools, approvals, permissions, and mission orchestration.

Automated test suite `test_headless_stage2a.py` passed all assertions including 401 Unauthorized rejection on unauthenticated bridge calls, same-origin Next.js SSE proxy relay, and full STT->NORA->TTS loop. ## 2026-10-04 12:45 +06:00 (Antigravity) — Real-Time CORE Reactivity, Web Audio Sampler & Subtitle Safe-Zone Fixes, UNCOMMITTED; awaiting review

Resolved real-time interaction chain across Dive In → Overview route (`growforge-ui`) with verified physical responsiveness in Three.js and structural subtitle layout fix. Preserved calibrated idle geometry, Overview cards, background, typography, metrics, navigation, and BRAIN visual architecture.
- **Root Causes Discovered & Fixed:**
  1. *Microphone Reaction Failure:* The browser `AudioContext` was suspended on initialization without `ctx.resume()`, and high noise-floor threshold (`0.035`) combined with sub-perceptual shader multipliers (`0.45` glow, `0.04` scale) completely masked real speech energy. Fixed by adding `ctx.resume()`, composite frequency-domain energy sampling (80Hz–4kHz), lowering noise floor to `0.012`, and boosting perceptual multipliers (`1.25` glow, `0.16` nucleus scale).
  2. *Idle Motion Imperceptibility:* Idle breathing wave frequency was set at `0.32` with amplitude `0.012` (~20s cycle of 1.2% change), appearing static. Fixed to organic 4.8s breathing period (`Math.sin(time * 1.3) * 0.035` = 3.5% amplitude) with continuous membrane refractive wave motion (`time * 0.18`).
  3. *Hover / Click Attentive Weakness:* Pointer nucleus shift was `0.035 * pointer.x` (<1px on canvas); click impulse scale was `0.018`. Fixed to clear `0.12 * pointer.x` (6–10px shift), `+0.35` glow surge, and full 0–800ms click wake sequence (0–150ms inward compression `-0.055`, 150–350ms nucleus brightening `+0.75`, 250–800ms outward shell wave `+0.55`).
  4. *Subtitle / Composer Collision:* In `OverviewRuntime.module.css`, `.volume:has(+.commandSurfaces)~.subtitle` calculated `top: calc(29% + min(520px,49vh) + 34px)` which collided directly with the bottom composer dock. Replaced with structured safe-zone positioning (`bottom: 172px; width: min(560px, calc(100% - 48px)); max-height: 52px; -webkit-line-clamp: 2`) strictly between CORE state pill and bottom composer dock.
- **Real Voice Energy Sampling:** Created browser-native Web Audio API analyser in `src/lib/audioSampler.ts` using `AudioContext` + `AnalyserNode` + `MediaStreamAudioSourceNode` via `navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ audio: true })`. Activated strictly upon user-initiated voice input (`recognition.onstart`). Clamps noise floor (`0.012`), smoothly normalizes RMS energy (silence: `0.000`, normal speech: `0.180–0.420`, loud speech: `0.550–0.780`, capped below `0.880`), and broadcasts via `growforge:nora-audio` event. On stop/error/cancel, releases all media tracks and closes context.
- **Deterministic State Precedence:** Strictly enforced in `innerCorePhase()`: `degraded` → `success` → `listening` → `speaking` (inactive future hook) → `thinking` → `responding` → `awaiting` → `executing` → `attentive` → `idle`.
- **Signal Blending & Dev Telemetry:** One final composed target per property before interpolation. Dev telemetry exposed on `window.__CORE_TELEMETRY` and conditional HUD via `?telemetry=1`.
- **Verification:** Full TypeScript (`tsc --noEmit`) 0 errors, focused ESLint on modified files 0 errors/0 warnings, production build (`npm run build`) 42 routes PASS. Executable test suite `scripts/test-core-reactivity.ts` and all existing dive/overview/security suites (18 suites) PASS. No commit, push, or deployment.
